Let's explore discrete algorithms more concretely. We have time 2400s, CPU. We can implement:
- A family of initial sequences: random monotone/profile, geometric, beta, two-sided, random smooth, random balanced, etc.
- Run a fast local search using gradient with q-smoothing and random restarts. Need exact evaluation to avoid non-symmetric? We'll keep high n? Choose n maybe 2048 or 4096. Convolution via FFT cost N≈8192 or16384. In Python, FFT 4096 ~0.001? More like 0.005. 10k evals =50s. Good. We can do many.
- Local update: instead of L-BFGS, use a custom active local optimization based on gradient of smoothed objective with adaptive step and random restarts. Could be more robust.

One simple method: minimize surrogate F_q with gradient descent in z using multiplicative updates and line search. Pseudocode:
1. Initialize z (e.g., from seed logits). p=softmax.
2. Compute c, y, max, q-smooth objective value.
3. Let weights w = y^{q-1} / (sum + eps). (Maybe use q increasing over time anneal: start q=2 or 4 to smooth, increase to 64/128 for max.)
4. Compute B=corr(w,p), H=B - dot(w,c). Direction for z: dz = -step * H? Or p*(B-C)? For softmax, use H or p*H? Let's derive for log objective with weights v (not normalized). Gradient true ∝ p (B-C). If we update z by -H, the change in p: if δ_i=-H_i, mean p change ≈ -H^T p + (H^T p)^2? Since H weighted mean zero: p_iH_i sum? p^T H = C - B^T? Actually H=B-C; p^T H= p^T B - C = C-C=0. So first order sum unchanged. Good! H has weighted zero mean, but not all entries. δ=-H. Then p change ≈ p_i H_i. This is same relative change as using gradient direction p*H but z step without p; relative change = η H, magnitude η *1/n. If η ~ O(1), relative changes small. We can set η adaptively to make max relative change ~alpha, e.g., η = alpha / (max|H|) so relative O(alpha). This may be good. Since H units ~1/n, η~n*alpha. Then p change ~alpha weights. We can line search: try η values (scale relative alphas 0.1,0.3,1,3,10). Update z ← z - step * H (or step = alpha / maxabs(H) maybe). After update, p. Check objective surrogate/exact; accept if better; else reduce alpha. This is like projected scaled gradient.
Alternatively use p_i *= exp(-H_i * eta) and normalize; but if H large, some p underflow. Use z update with cap step*|H|≤logcap (e.g., 5) to keep p positive.
- Randomize: at each iteration, if no improvement, perturb z (small noise) to escape.
- Coarse-to-fine q: start q=2 or 4 (smooth Lp) to reduce peaks, then q=16,32,64,128.
- Periodically reseed if stalled.

Pseudo:
```
def spectral_refine(p, n, N, target_y, budget):
    best_p=p; best_y=score(p)
    cap_y = best_y
    improvement_count=0
    while time left and cap_y > target_y:
        # for each cap, do 30 iterations
        for it in range(30):
            c = conv(p); y=2n*c; max_y=max(y)
            c_target = np.minimum(c, cap_y/(2n))
            s=c_target.sum()
            if s<1e-12: c_target=c; s=1
            ct = c_target/s
            P = sqrt(rfft(ct,N))
            p_real = irfft(P,N)[:n]
            p1=clip(p_real,0); p2=abs(p_real)
            candidates = concat p1,p2, maybe 0.7p+0.3p1
            for cand normalize; score; choose best among candidates and old? We can keep current p as one candidate and update to the one with best exact (or maybe if exact worsens, still move to best surrogate? spectral deterministic; choose best exact maybe greedily; if no improvement, lower cap? Actually spectral hard may oscillate; use next_best = min candidate score. If next_best <= best_y + small, accept as current; else keep current.
            if next_best < best_y: update best.
        # reduce cap
        cap_y = max(target_y, best_y*0.95 or cap_y*0.9?)
```

def lp_step(p, n, M, U=10.0):
    m=2*n-1
    rows = []? Build sparse.
    from scipy.sparse import lil_matrix? For n=1024, use lil.
    A = lil_matrix((m,n))
    for i,f in enumerate(p):
       if f<=0: continue
       # terms k from i to i+n-1: A[k,i]=f[i]
       if f !=0:
          A[i:i+n, i] = f  # slice assignment in lil maybe works?
    A = A.tocsr()
    b = np.full(m, M)
    bounds=(0,U)
    res = linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method='highs')
```

Let's design exact score function with reusing N:
```
def _next_n(n):
    m=2*n-1
    N=1
    while N<m: N*=2
    return N

def _conv(p, N=None):
    n=p.size
    if N is None: N=_next_n(n)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(P*P,N)[:2*n-1]
    # remove tiny neg
    c[c<0] = 0? For exact max, tiny negative should not matter; set max(0).
    return c, P, N
```
But if we set negative to0, sum c changes tiny; max unaffected. For score, take max.
```
def _score_from_conv(p,c):
    return float(2*p.size*np.max(c))
```

Spectral refine implementation details:
```
def _spectral_refine(p, n, N, target_y, deadline, max_iter):
   best=p.copy(); best_y=_score(p)
   cap_y = max(target_y, _score(p)*0.99)
   no_improve=0
   mode toggles
   while time<deadline and no_improve<100:
       c, Pff, N = _conv(p)
       max_y=2n max c
       if max_y <= target_y: break
       # choose cap from cap_y maybe
       cap_coeff = cap_y/(2n)
       c_target = np.minimum(c, cap_coeff)
       # sometimes use power flatten
       if (iter%5==4): c_target = c ** alpha? But c near0; use log.
       s = c_target.sum()
       if s < 0.5: # too clipped, use softer: c_target = c * cap_y/(max_y+eps)?
           c_target = c * (cap_y/(max_y+1e-12))
           s=sum
       ct=c_target/s
       Ct = np.fft.rfft(ct, N)
       Pnew = np.sqrt(Ct + 0j)
       p_real = np.fft.irfft(Pnew,N)[:n]
       candidates=[]
       for proj in [np.clip(p_real,0,1), np.abs(p_real)]:
          proj = np.clip(proj,0,1)
          s=proj.sum(); if s>1e-12: candidates.append(proj/s)
       # blend with current to avoid jumps
       for proj in ...:
           blended=0.5*best? Actually current? use 0.3 current +0.7 cand
       evaluate each; keep best score among candidates and current.
       if best_score_new < best_y: update current/best; no_improve=0
       else: no_improve+=1
       # update cap_y
       if best_y <= cap_y*(1+0.01): cap_y = max(target_y, cap_y*0.95)
       else: cap_y = max(target_y, cap_y*0.98 or best_y*0.99)
   return best,best_y
```
Power flatten: For c, choose alpha = 0.75,0.6. `ct = c**alpha;` normalize. This does not hard cap but flattens. Can alternate with cap. For alpha, use log: `ct=np.exp(alpha * np.log(np.maximum(c,1e-300)))`.

Let's also include a simple "diagonal balancing" heuristic:
Given p and current c, identify max lag k. We can perform a linear programming / gradient move: For that lag, paired values r_i=p_{k-i}. To reduce c while preserving sum, transfer mass from indices i where r_i high to j where r_j low, but only if the transfer also doesn't increase other max too much. We can use pairwise swaps: choose i high paired, j low paired, move small mass from i to j. This is like coordinate optimization. Implementing may be messy. Gradient steps cover.

We can also use current c and weights to approximate score by active max? But exact needed.

Pseudo for gradient phase:
```
def _gradient_refine(p, n, N, deadline, target_y):
    best=p.copy(); best_y=score
    current=p.copy(); current_y=best_y
    stall=0
    q_list=[4,8,16,32,64] maybe start 4.
    for q in q_list:
        for it in range(1000):
            if time>deadline: break
            c=Pff...
            y=2n*c
            max_y=... if max_y==0 break
            logw=...
            w=...
            B=...
            H=B-C
            maxH=np.max(np.abs(H))
            if maxH<1e-15: break
            alphas = np.array([0.05,0.15,0.4,1.0]) if q>16 else [0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0]
            best_cand=current; best_cand_y=current_y
            for a in alphas:
                cand = current*np.exp(- (a/maxH)*H)
                if not finite or sum<=0: continue
                cand/=sum
                candidate_score=eval
                if candidate_score < best_cand_y - 1e-12:
                   best_cand=cand; best_cand_y=candidate_score
            if best_cand_y < current_y*(1-1e-9) or stall>20:
                current=best_cand; current_y=best_cand_y; stall=0
                if current_y<best_y: best=current; best_y=current_y
            else:
                stall +=1
                if stall>30:
                   current = random_perturb(current,rng,sigma=0.2)
                   current_y=score(current); stall=0
    return best,best_y
```
Maybe step direction sign: We want reduce objective. If H=B-C. True gradient wrt z = p(H) (positive to increase F). Descent z -= η H. So p multiply exp(-η H). If H positive, decrease p; if H negative, increase. Good.
Check with uniform q=2 H values: B0 low <C => H negative at endpoints -> increase endpoints; B1 high -> H positive -> decrease middle? This makes U-shaped symmetric, lowering L2. For max objective, maybe not immediate, but may open non-sym? If seed already asym, good.

We can include a small asymmetry bias? Since symmetric directions can increase max. Our seeds asym, gradient may maintain asym. Good.

Objective unaffected tiny. But nonnegative entries can be zero; evaluator okay. LP needs >0. `np.fft.rfft(p, N)` times itself -> circular convolution of p with p length N. If N≥2n-1, coefficients 0..2n-2 linear. Because p zero-padded beyond n-1. Good.

Numpy FFT: X_k=Σ_{n=0}^{N-1} x_n e^{-2πi kn/N}. ifft(Y)=1/NΣ Y_k e^{+2πi kn/N}. Let A=W, B=p. C=irfft(FA * conj(FB)). Time c_k =1/NΣ A_hat_k conj(B_hat_k) e^{2πikn/N}. Let's implement `_normalize`:
```
def _normalize(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64)
    if p.ndim!=1 or size==0 or not finite: return None
    p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
    s=p.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12: return None
    p=p/s
    # floor
    floor = 1e-16
    p=np.maximum(p, floor)
    p=p/p.sum()
    return p
```
Floor adds tiny mass. For p scores, tiny.

`_score`:
```
def _score(p,N=None):
    p=np.asarray(p)
    n=p.size
    if n==0 or not finite all: return inf
    s=p.sum(); if s<1e-6: return inf
    p=p/s
    if N is None: N=_fft_len(n)
    c,_,_=... but _convolution returns P; for score use np.fft.
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,N); c=np.fft.irfft(P*P,N)[:2*n-1]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return float(2*n*np.max(c))
```
For many calls, doing normalization copy. We'll assume p already normalized; use `_fast_score(p,N)` requiring finite normalized.
```
def _fast_score(p,N):
   n=p.size; P=np.fft.rfft(p,N); c=irfft(P*P,N)[:2*n-1]; return 2*n*max(c)
```
Use `np.max(c)`; if c tiny negative, max maybe negative? p positive sum, c0 positive, so max positive. Use `max(float(...),0)`.

`_normalize`:
```
def _normalize(p, floor=None):
    try: p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64)
    except: return None
    if p.ndim!=1 or p.size==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(p)): return None
    p=np.clip(p,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(p.sum())
    if s<1e-12: return None
    p=p/s
    if floor is None: floor=max(1e-16, 1e-18*p.size)??
    p=np.maximum(p, floor)
    p=p/p.sum()
    return p
```
Don't floor too large: floor=1e-15/n maybe. Use `floor=1e-15` maybe if n large 8192 gives min mass 1e-15, negligible.

score=float(_fast_score(p,N))
return score,p
```

```
def _spectral_project(ct, n, N):
    C=np.fft.fft(ct, N)
    P=np.sqrt(C + 0j)
    pc=np.fft.ifft(P)
    cands=[]
    r=pc.real
    cands.append(np.clip(r,0,None))
    cands.append(np.abs(r))
    # maybe magnitude
    cands.append(np.abs(pc))
    best=None; best_score=inf
    for cand in cands:
       cand=_normalize(cand)
       if cand is None: continue
       sc=_fast_score(cand,N)
       if sc<best_score: best=cand; best_score=sc
    return best, best_score
```
mgiration: normalized ct sum1. `ct` might have negative tiny from c? We ensure nonnegative.

`_spectral_candidate(p, n, N, target_y, cap_y, alpha=None)`:
```
def _spectral_candidate(p, n, N, target_y, cap_y, power_alpha=None, mode='cap'):
   Pfft=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
   c=np.fft.irfft(Pfft*Pfft,N)[:2*n-1]
   c=np.maximum(c,0)
   # scale coef target = cap_y/(2n)
   if mode=='cap':
      cap_coeff = cap_y/(2*n)
      ct=np.minimum(c, cap_coeff)
      s=ct.sum()
      if s < 1e-12:
          ct=c
      else:
          # if normalized max exceeds cap maybe do iterative water fill?
          ct=ct/s
   elif mode=='power':
      # flatten: ct = c**alpha normalized
      small=1e-300
      logs=np.where(c>small, np.log(c), np.log(small))
      ct=np.exp(power_alpha*logs)
      s=ct.sum(); ct=ct/s
   # optional: ensure area sum1
   return _spectral_project(ct,n,N)
```
But if power_alpha<1, edges (small) increase; maybe good.
Maybe combine: if mode='cap_power': ct=np.minimum(c, cap_coeff) then apply power? no.

def _spectral_refine(p, n, N, deadline, target_y, max_iters=None):
    p=_normalize(p)
    current=p.copy(); best=p.copy(); best_y=_fast_score(p,N); current_y=best_y
    if max_iters is None: max_iters = 1000
    no_improve=0
    # if target_y < lower_bound, set
    lower = 2.0*n/(2*n-1)
    target_y = max(target_y, lower + 1e-6)
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       # optional recompute current_y if not from last? We have best.
       cur_ycurrent = best_y? use best to start each iteration to avoid drift.

LP indexing cache:
```
def _get_lp_index(n):
   if n in _LP_CACHE: return
   rows=[]; cols=[]; data=[]; # data depends p, so indices only
   row = np.arange(n, dtype=int)
   for i in range(n):
      r = i + row
      c = np.full(n, i, dtype=int)
      rows.append(r); cols.append(c)
   rows=np.concatenate(rows); cols=np.concatenate(cols)
   _LP_CACHE[n]=(rows,cols)
   return rows,cols
```

Main refine per seed:
```
def _refine_sequence(p, n, N, deadline, rng):

```
def _polish_budget(p,n,N,rng,budget,target_y):
   start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+budget
   # spectral 40%
   t1 = min(deadline, start + budget*0.4)
   p1,y1 = _spectral_refine(p,n,N,t1,target_y,rng)
   # gradient 40%
   rem = deadline - max(time, t1)
   t2 = min(deadline, time + rem*0.6)
   p2,y2 = _gradient_refine(p1,n,N,t2,target_y,rng)
   # spectral final 20%
   if time < deadline:
      p3,y3 = _spectral_refine(p2,n,N,deadline,target_y,rng)
      if y3<y2: p2,y2=p3,y3
   return p2,y2
```

def _random_perturb(p, rng, sigma=0.1):

Let's write `_gradient_refine` with internal q loop but share time. Pseudocode full:
```
def _gradient_refine(p, n, N, deadline, target_y, rng):
    current = _normalize(p); current_y=_fast_score(current,N)
    best=current.copy(); best_y=current_y
    stall=0
    q_list = [4,8,16,32,64]
    it_total=0
    for q in q_list:
        alphas = np.array([0.05,0.15,0.4,1.0,2.0]) if q <= 8 else np.array([0.02,0.06,0.15,0.4,1.0])
        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
            it_total +=1
            Pfft=np.fft.rfft(current,N)
            c=np.fft.irfft(Pfft*Pfft,N)[:2*n-1]
            c=np.maximum(c,0)
            y=2*n*c
            max_y=float(np.max(y))
            if not finite: break
            # weights
            if q == 1: weights uniform? skip
            logs... w
            B=irfft(rfft(w,N)*conj(Pfft),N)[:n]
            Cterm=np.dot(w,c)
            H=B-Cterm
            maxH=float(np.max(np.abs(H)))
            if maxH < 1e-14: break
            best_cand=current; best_cand_y=current_y
            for a in alphas:
                eta=a/maxH
                delta=-eta*H
                # if a small, no clip? delta within [-a,a]; for safety:
                delta=np.clip(delta,-50,50)
                cand=current*np.exp(delta)
                cand=np.maximum(cand,1e-15)
                s=cand.sum()
                if s<=0 or not finite: continue
                cand=cand/s
                sc=float(_fast_score(cand,N))
                if sc < best_cand_y - 1e-12:
                   best_cand=cand; best_cand_y=sc
            # accept rule
            if best_cand_y < current_y * (1-1e-10):
                current=best_cand; current_y=best_cand_y; stall=0
            elif best_cand_y < current_y + 0.02 and it_total % 3 ==0:
                current=best_cand; current_y=best_cand_y
            else:
                stall +=1
                if stall > 50:
                    current=_random_perturb(current,rng,0.2)
                    current_y=float(_fast_score(current,N))
                    stall=0
            if current_y < best_y:
                best=current.copy(); best_y=current_y
            # if best_y <= target_y and maybe no progress, break? Continue try lower.
    return best,best_y
```
This may spend too much on current if not improving; after each q no improvement, break next q? If stall resets by random perturb, may wander. We can cap total iterations per q? Use `iterations_per_q = max(50, min(500, int((deadline-time)*10)))`? Simpler while time but if best_y not improved for many iterations across all q, could loop. `it_total` and `no_best` count: if `no_best>200` break. Implement `no_best_total=0`; in each iteration if best_y improved set0 else++. If no_best_total>200 or q loop done break.

mlog=np.max(logw)
w=np.exp(logw-mlog)
s=w.sum()
if s>0: w/=s
else: w=np.full_like(c,1.0/(2*n-1))
```

Let's implement `_spectral_refine` with current/best and no external target iterations. Code:
```
def _spectral_refine(p,n,N,deadline,target_y,rng):
    current=_normalize(p); current_y=score; best=current; best_y=current_y
    lower=2*n/(2*n-1)
    target_y=max(target_y, lower + 1e-6)
    no_best=0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it+=1
       # base maybe current
       base=current
       base_y=current_y
       frac = 0.97 if it < 20 else (0.99 if base_y - target_y > 0.05 else 0.997)
       cap_y=max(target_y, base_y*frac)
       # choose mode
       if it % 4 == 0:
           alpha = 0.75 - 0.05*min(it/50,3)
           cand,cyd = _spectral_candidate(base,n,N,target_y,cap_y,power_alpha=alpha,mode='power')
       else:
           cand,cyd = _spectral_candidate(base,n,N,target_y,cap_y,mode='cap')
       if cand is not None and cyd < inf:
          # accept candidate if better than current or small anneal
          if cyd < current_y or (cyd < current_y + 0.03 and it % 2 ==0):
              current=cand; current_y=cyd
          if cyd < best_y:
              best=cand; best_y=cyd; no_best=0
          else:
              no_best+=1
          if no_best > 60:
              # perturb best and set current
              current=_random_perturb(best,rng,0.15); current_y=score
              no_best=0
       if best_y <= target_y*0.98 and it>20: # maybe still continue to lower but break?
           pass
       if no_best > 300: break
    return best,best_y
```

```

blend=_normalize(0.5*base+0.5*cand); sb=score; if sb<score: best_cand=blend...
```

`_resample_length` loop details:
```
def _resample_length(p,n_out):
    p=_normalize(p)
    if p is None: return None
    m=p.size
    f=m*p
    out=np.zeros(n_out)
    old_edges=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m+1)
    new_edges=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_out+1)
    j=0
    for i in range(n_out):
        a=new_edges[i]; b=new_edges[i+1]
        while j<m and old_edges[j+1] <= a + 1e-15:
            j+=1
        k=j
        while k<m and old_edges[k] < b - 1e-15:
            ov = min(b, old_edges[k+1]) - max(a, old_edges[k])
            if ov > 0:
                out[i] += f[k]*ov
            k += 1
    # j remains? If we set k increments, next interval j should be max previous k? We can set j=k after loop.
    j=k if k<=m else m
    s=out.sum()
    if s<=0: return None
    return out/s
```
But in inner while, k should not advance beyond b; set j = max(j, k) perhaps. This is O(m+n)? For each new interval, k starts at j and may advance. Since new intervals increase, total k advances ≤m plus maybe resets? Actually k can advance to b, next interval starts at a'=b and j should be the first old edge >=a'. Setting j=k works if old_edges[k] maybe >=a'? If old_edges[k-1] was <a', okay. But if multiple old edges <=a', while loop at top handles. Total O(m+n). Good.
If m=1, f=[1], out all1? f=1, overlaps length1/n each => out sum1. Good.

`_block_from_heights`:
```
def _heights_to_p(H,n):
    K=len(H)
    idx=np.minimum(K-1, (np.arange(n)*K//n).astype(np.int64))
    p=H[idx]/float(n)
    return _normalize(p)
```
If H sum K, p sum approx1.

Let's write top-level `_fft_len` efficient:
```
def _fft_len(n):
    m = 2*n -1
    N=1
    while N < m: N <<=1
    return N
```

`_normalize`:
```
def _normalize(p):
    try:
       arr=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
       return None
    if arr.ndim !=1 or arr.size ==0:
       return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
       return None
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-14:
       return None
    arr=arr/s
    floor=1e-16
    arr=np.maximum(arr,floor)
    arr=arr/arr.sum()
    return arr
```
Floor could make sum slightly >1; renormalize.

`_fast_score`:
```
def _fast_score(p,N):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64)
    n=p.size
    if n==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(p)): return np.inf
    s=float(np.sum(p))
    if s <=1e-14: return np.inf
    # We can use raw p; but if p has negatives? use clip? We'll assume nonneg; for safety
    if np.min(p) < -1e-12:
        p=np.clip(p,0.0,1000.0); s=float(np.sum(p))
        if s<=1e-14: return np.inf
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(P*P,N)
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    mx=float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(mx) or mx < 0.0:
        mx=max(0.0,mx)
    return float(2.0*n*mx/(s*s))
```
If mx negative due all zero? return 0? But p sum>0, c0 positive; set max(0). If s small but okay.

`_safe_score` = normalize and fast.

def _heights_to_p(H,n):

    if H.size==0 or n<=0: return None
    H=np.clip(H,0.0,1e6)
    sh=H.sum()
    if sh<=0: return None
    H = sh? We want sum H maybe arbitrary; p = H[idx]/(sh) * H.size? Actually if H not sum K, p_i = H_j / (K? sumH?) Let's make p_i proportional to H_j with equal block counts. Sum p = Σ_j count_j H_j / S? Let S=H.sum(); set p_i = H_j / (S * n / K?) Need exact: We want sum p=1. If p_i = H_j * c, sum = c Σ_j H_j count_j. If counts approximately n/K not exact for last. Easiest: p_i = H_{idx}; then normalize. So `p = H[idx]; return _normalize(p)`. Good; sum not pre. No need K. If H spiky, okay.
```

`_random_block_seed`:
```
def _random_block_seed(n,K,rng):
    z=np.zeros(K)
    rho=0.8+0.2*rng.random()
    noise=0.5+1.5*rng.random()
    z[0]=rng.normal(0,1.0)
    for j in range(1,K): z[j]=rho*z[j-1]+rng.normal(0,noise)
    trend=rng.uniform(-1,1)
    z += trend * np.linspace(-1,1,K)
    if rng.random()<0.5:
       z += rng.uniform(0.5,2.0)*np.sin(2*np.pi*rng.random()*np.arange(K)/K + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
    z -= np.max(z)
    H=np.exp(z)
    H[H<=0]=1e-12
    H = H / H.sum() * float(K) # sum K not needed
    return _heights_to_p(H,n)
```
Trend: If trend positive, z larger at end (increasing). Negative decreasing. Good.

def _power_seed(n, alpha, decreasing=False):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    if decreasing: x=1.0-x
    p=x**alpha
    return _normalize(p)
```
alpha can be negative? Avoid singular. Use positive.

def _step_seed(n, a, left_mass):
    if n<2: ...
    L=int(round(a*n))
    L=min(max(L,1),n-1)
    p=np.zeros(n)
    p[:L]=left_mass/L
    p[L:]=(1-left_mass)/(n-L)
    return _normalize(p)
```

Spectral functions:
```
def _spectral_project(ct,n,N):
   ct=np.asarray(ct,dtype=np.float64)
   ct=np.maximum(ct,0.0)
   s=ct.sum()
   if s<=1e-14: return None, np.inf
   ct=ct/s
   C=np.fft.fft(ct,N)
   P=np.sqrt(C + 0j)
   pc=np.fft.ifft(P)
   best=None; best_sc=np.inf
   for arr in (pc.real, np.abs(pc.real), np.abs(pc)):
       cand=_normalize(arr)
       if cand is None: continue
       sc=_fast_score(cand,N)
       if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best=cand
   return best,best_sc
```
`np.abs(pc)` maybe length N; first n? The inverse Fourier gives length N, but the first n elements are the sequence; the rest wrap/circular due target length? Wait if Pfreq = sqrt(FFT(ct,N)), the inverse ifft has N entries; we want a length-n sequence P whose square gives ct (zero-padded). The sequence should be the first n entries; the entries n..N-1 should be zero ideally but may have leakage/wring due square root causing zeros. We must truncate to n before normalization. In `_spectral_project`, do `pc=pc[:n]` then candidates. Good. Same for gradient B.
```
pc = np.fft.ifft(P, N)[:n]
```
Then candidates from pc length n.

`_spectral_candidate`:
```
def _spectral_candidate(p,n,N,target_y,cap_y,power_alpha=None,mode='cap'):
   Pfft=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
   c=np.fft.irfft(Pfft*Pfft,N)[:2*n-1]
   c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
   if mode=='power' and power_alpha is not None:
       small=1e-300
       logc=np.log(np.maximum(c, small))
       ct=np.exp(power_alpha*logc)
   else:
       lower=2.0*n/(2*n-1)
       cap_y=max(cap_y, lower)
       cap_coeff=cap_y/(2.0*n)
       ct=np.minimum(c, cap_coeff)
       s=ct.sum()
       if s < 1e-12:
           ct=c
       # else normalize done in project
   cand,sc=_spectral_project(ct,n,N)
   return cand,sc
```
If power ct includes zeros? If c has many zeros, log small; ct small but normalized. Good.

`_spectral_refine` as above. Need `_random_perturb` defined.

def _random_perturb(p, rng, sigma=0.15):
   z=rng.normal(0.0,sigma,size=p.size)
   cand=p*np.exp(z)
   cand=np.maximum(cand,1e-16)
   return _normalize(cand)
```

`_spectral_refine` code robust:
```
def _spectral_refine(p, n, N, deadline, target_y, rng):
    current=_normalize(p)
    if current is None: return p, _fast_score(p,N) if p is not None else np.inf
    current_y=float(_fast_score(current,N))
    best=current.copy(); best_y=current_y
    lower=2.0*n/(2*n-1)
    target_y=max(float(target_y), lower+1e-6)
    no_best=0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        base=current
        base_y=current_y
        frac = 0.97 if it < 30 else (0.99 if base_y - target_y > 0.05 else 0.997)
        cap_y = max(target_y, base_y * frac)
        if it % 4 == 0:
            alpha = 0.8 - 0.1*min(float(it)/100.0, 2.0)
            cand,cyd = _spectral_candidate(base,n,N,target_y,cap_y,power_alpha=alpha,mode='power')
        else:
            cand,cyd = _spectral_candidate(base,n,N,target_y,cap_y,mode='cap')
        if cand is not None and math.isfinite(cyd):
            # also try blend every 3?
            blend_cand=None
            if it % 3 == 0:
                blend=_normalize(0.5*base + 0.5*cand)
                if blend is not None:
                    bs=_fast_score(blend,N)
                    if bs < cyd:
                        cand, cyd, blend_cand = blend, bs, blend
            if cyd < current_y or (cyd < current_y + 0.05 and it % 3 == 0):
                current=cand; current_y=cyd
            if cyd < best_y:
                best=cand; best_y=cyd; no_best=0
            else:
                no_best += 1
            if no_best > 40:
                pert=_random_perturb(best, rng, 0.15)
                if pert is not None:
                    current=pert; current_y=float(_fast_score(current,N))
                no_best=0
        else:
            no_best += 1
        if no_best > 300:
            break
        if best_y <= (target_y - 1e-6) and it > 50:
            # continue for a while to push lower, but if no best 100 break
            pass
    return best,best_y
```
If nothing improves, current stays best; blend maybe. Good.

`_gradient_refine` code with timing. Need ensure no lambdas. We'll compute candidates in list maybe.
```
def _gradient_refine(p, n, N, deadline, target_y, rng):
    current=_normalize(p)
    current_y=float(_fast_score(current,N))
    best=current.copy(); best_y=current_y
    no_best=0
    q_list=[4,8,16,32,64]
    it=0
    for q in q_list:
        alphas = [0.05,0.15,0.4,1.0,2.0] if q <= 8 else [0.02,0.06,0.15,0.4,1.0]
        # maybe time per q: while time and (no_best<150)
        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
            it +=1
            if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
            Pfft=np.fft.rfft(current,N)
            c=np.fft.irfft(Pfft*Pfft,N)[:2*n-1]
            c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
            y=2.0*n*c
            max_y=float(np.max(y))
            if not math.isfinite(max_y) or max_y <= 0: break
            small=1e-300
            logw=(q-1.0)*np.log(np.maximum(y, small))
            mlog=float(np.max(logw))
            w=np.exp(logw-mlog)
            sw=float(np.sum(w))
            if sw <=0 or not math.isfinite(sw):
                w=np.full(c.shape, 1.0/c.shape[0])
            else:
                w=w/sw
            Wfft=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
            B=np.fft.irfft(Wfft*np.conj(Pfft), N)[:n]
            Cterm=float(np.dot(w,c))
            H=B-Cterm
            maxH=float(np.max(np.abs(H)))
            if not math.isfinite(maxH) or maxH < 1e-14:
                break
            best_cand=current; best_cand_y=current_y
            for a in alphas:
                if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
                delta=- (float(a)/maxH) * H
                # if large, clip
                if float(a) > 20: delta=np.clip(delta,-50,50) # alphas small
                cand=current*np.exp(delta)
                cand=np.maximum(cand,1e-16)
                s=float(np.sum(cand))
                if s<=0 or not math.isfinite(s): continue
                cand=cand/s
                sc=float(_fast_score(cand,N))
                if sc < best_cand_y - 1e-12:
                    best_cand=cand; best_cand_y=sc
            if best_cand_y < current_y * (1.0 - 1e-10):
                current=best_cand; current_y=best_cand_y
            elif best_cand_y < current_y + 0.05 and it % 4 == 0:
                current=best_cand; current_y=best_cand_y
            else:
                no_best += 10
            if current_y < best_y - 1e-12:
                best=current.copy(); best_y=current_y; no_best=0
            elif no_best > 40:
                # if no overall improvement, try random perturb
                pert=_random_perturb(current,rng,0.2)
                if pert is not None:
                    current=pert; current_y=float(_fast_score(current,N))
                if no_best > 200: break
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
        # If best score hasn't improved across q, and no_best large, maybe stop
        if best_y <= target_y*0.98 and q in [32,64]:
            # optional continue?
            pass
        if no_best > 500: break
    return best,best_y
```
This `no_best` is shared; if random perturb resets? We set no_best not reset on perturb; maybe if pert random but no improvement, break. Good.
Potential issue: in gradient, accepting if best_cand_y < current_y + 0.05 can move to worse, but if no improvement best stays. Could spiral; no_best counts? We lower no_best only if current_y improves overall best. If accepting small worsening, no_best accumulates until perturb. OK.
Maybe use `alphas` 5 candidates; candidate score 5 FFTs; if time budget small, overhead. Good.

`_polish_budget`:
```
def _polish_budget(p,n,N,rng,budget,target_y):
    p=_normalize(p)
    if p is None: return None,float('inf')
    score=float(_fast_score(p,N))
    if not math.isfinite(score): return p,score
    start=time.monotonic()
    # if budget very small, return
    # spectral first portion
    t1=min(start+budget, start + 0.45*budget)
    p1,y1=_spectral_refine(p,n,N,t1,target_y,rng)
    y2=y1
    # gradient
    t2=min(start+budget, max(time.monotonic(), time.monotonic() + 0.4*budget))
    # Better: t2=time.monotonic()+0.4*budget, min end
    end=start+budget
    t2=min(end, time.monotonic()+0.4*budget)
    p2,y2=_gradient_refine(p1,n,N,t2,target_y,rng)
    # final spectral if time
    if time.monotonic() < end - 1.0:
        p3,y3=_spectral_refine(p2,n,N,end,target_y,rng)
        if y3 < y2:
            p2,y2=p3,y3
    return p2,y2
```
But if spectral used 0.45*budget, gradient uses 0.4*budget from now, final up to end. Good.
If spectral improves best, gradient uses p1. If spectral returns best, okay.

We should track best inside `_polish_budget`? `_spectral_refine` returns best candidate from p1; `_gradient_refine` returns best after gradient (including p1). Good.

Potential issue: `_spectral_refine` has target_y maybe lower than score; it may run until t1 but if no improve, returns original. Good.

Define:

def _choose_target(score,n):
   lower=2.0*n/(2*n-1)+0.02
   if not math.isfinite(score): return max(1.45, lower)
   if score >= 1.9: return max(1.65, lower)
   if score >= 1.7: return max(1.55, lower)
   if score >= 1.55: return max(1.45, lower)
   if score >= 1.45: return max(1.38, lower)
   return max(1.30, lower)
```

# polish top 3 with budget according to remaining

```
def _block_objective(z,n,N,q):
    K=z.size
    H=_softmax_heights(z,K)
    p=_heights_to_p(H,n)
    if p is None: return 1e6
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,N); c=np.fft.irfft(P*P,N)[:2*n-1]; c=np.maximum(c,0)
    y=2*n*c
    L=y.size
    logy=np.log(np.maximum(y,1e-300))
    v=q*logy
    m=float(np.max(v))
    if not math.isfinite(m): return 1e6
    s=float(np.sum(np.exp(v-m)))
    if s<=0 or not math.isfinite(s): return 1e6
    return float(math.exp((m - math.log(s? wait)??)/q - math.log(L)/q))
```

def _sort_pool(pool, limit):
   if not pool: return []
   scores=np.array([s for s,p in pool])
   order=np.argsort(scores, kind='stable')
   return [pool[int(i)] for i in order[:limit]]
```

`_build_pool`:
```
def _build_pool(n, rng, parent_ref, num_random):
    N = _fft_len(n)
    pool=[]
    _append_seed(pool, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), N)
    if parent_ref is not None:
        _append_seed(pool, _resample_length(parent_ref, n), N)
    alphas=(0.2,0.35,0.5,0.7,1.0,1.5,2.2,3.0)
    for a in alphas:
        _append_seed(pool, _power_seed(n,a,False), N)
        _append_seed(pool, _power_seed(n,a,True), N)
    steps=( (0.5,0.333333),(0.5,0.666667),(0.35,0.25),(0.35,0.55),(0.65,0.75),(0.65,0.45) )
    for a,lm in steps:
        _append_seed(pool, _step_seed(n,a,lm), N)
    random_counts = max(0, int(num_random))
    K_choices=(8,12,16,24)
    if random_counts>0:
       for K in K_choices:
          for _ in range(max(1, random_counts//len(K_choices))):
              _append_seed(pool, _random_block_seed(n,K,rng), N)
    return pool, N
```

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative step-function heights minimizing

    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2

The program combines:
1. Several analytic/random seeds (powers, stochastic blocks, step functions).
2. A low-dimensional block-profile optimizer (unconstrained Nelder-Mead on softmax
   block heights, if scipy is available).
3. Nonlocal spectral projection: build a capped/flattened target convolution,
   take its polynomial square-root in the frequency domain, and project back to
   nonnegative time sequences.
4. Smoothed-max gradient descent in log-height variables, with adaptive steps
   and occasional random restarts.
5. Progressive polishing at larger lengths (2048 / 4096 / 8192 as time permits).
"""

import time
import math
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None


def _fft_len(n):
    """Smallest power of two at least 2*n - 1."""
    m = 2 * int(n) - 1
    N = 1
    while N < m:
        N <<= 1
    return N


def _normalize(p):
    """Clip, normalize to sum 1, and add a tiny floor to avoid exact zeros."""
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.ndim != 1 or arr.size == 0:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        return None
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-14:
        return None
    arr = arr / s
    floor = 1e-16
    arr = np.maximum(arr, floor)
    arr = arr / float(arr.sum())
    return arr


def _fast_score(p, N):
    """
    Exact objective for a raw nonnegative vector p, scale invariant:
        2*n*max(conv(p,p)) / sum(p)^2
    """
    try:
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    n = p.size
    if n <= 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
        return np.inf
    if float(np.min(p)) < -1e-12:
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 1e-14:
        return np.inf

    P = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(P * P, N)
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(mx) or mx < 0.0:
        mx = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * mx / (s * s))


def _safe_score(p):
    """Score after normalizing to a valid probability vector."""
    q = _normalize(p)
    if q is None:
        return np.inf
    return _fast_score(q, _fft_len(q.size))


def _append_seed(pool, p, N):
    q = _normalize(p)
    if q is None:
        return
    sc = _fast_score(q, N)
    if math.isfinite(sc):
        pool.append((float(sc), q.copy()))


def _sort_pool(pool, limit):
    if not pool:
        return []
    scores = np.array([float(s) for s, _ in pool], dtype=np.float64)
    order = np.argsort(scores)
    out = []
    for idx in order[:limit]:
        out.append(pool[int(idx)])
    return out


def _resample_length(p, n_out):
    """
    Resample a probability vector p to length n_out by treating p as a
    piecewise-constant density on equal cells and integrating over new cells.
    """
    q = _normalize(p)
    if q is None:
        return None
    m = q.size
    f = float(m) * q
    old_edges = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m + 1)
    new_edges = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n_out) + 1)
    out = np.zeros(int(n_out), dtype=np.float64)
    j = 0
    for i in range(int(n_out)):
        a = float(new_edges[i])
        b = float(new_edges[i + 1])
        while j < m and old_edges[j + 1] <= a + 1e-15:
            j += 1
        k = j
        while k < m and old_edges[k] < b - 1e-15:
            ov = min(b, old_edges[k + 1]) - max(a, old_edges[k])
            if ov > 0.0:
                out[i] += f[k] * ov
            k += 1
        if k > j:
            j = k
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 1e-14:
        return None
    return out / s


def _heights_to_p(H, n):
    """Turn relative block heights H into a length-n probability vector."""
    H = np.asarray(H, dtype=np.float64)
    if H.size == 0 or n <= 0:
        return None
    H = np.clip(H, 0.0, 1e6)
    if float(H.sum()) <= 1e-18:
        return None
    K = H.size
    idx = np.minimum(K - 1, (np.arange(int(n), dtype=np.int64) * K) // int(n))
    return _normalize(H[idx])


def _power_seed(n, alpha, decreasing=False):
    x = (np.arange(int(n), dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    if decreasing:
        x = 1.0 - x
    return _normalize(np.power(np.maximum(x, 1e-12), float(alpha)))


def _step_seed(n, a, left_mass):
    n = int(n)
    if n < 2:
        return None
    a = min(max(float(a), 1e-4), 1.0 - 1e-4)
    left_mass = min(max(float(left_mass), 1e-4), 1.0 - 1e-4)
    L = int(round(a * n))
    L = min(max(L, 1), n - 1)
    p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p[:L] = left_mass / float(L)
    p[L:] = (1.0 - left_mass) / float(n - L)
    return _normalize(p)


def _random_block_seed(n, K, rng):
    K = int(max(2, K))
    z = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    rho = 0.75 + 0.25 * float(rng.random())
    noise = 0.5 + 1.5 * float(rng.random())
    z[0] = float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0))
    for j in range(1, K):
        z[j] = rho * z[j - 1] + float(rng.normal(0.0, noise))

    trend = float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0))
    z += trend * np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, K)

    if rng.random() < 0.6:
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.4, 2.0))
        freq = int(rng.integers(1, 5))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        t = np.arange(K, dtype=np.float64) / float(K)
        z += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freq * t + phase)

    z -= float(np.max(z))
    H = np.exp(z)
    H = np.maximum(H, 1e-12)
    H = H / float(H.sum()) * float(K)
    return _heights_to_p(H, n)


def _build_pool(n, rng, parent_ref, num_random):
    N = _fft_len(n)
    pool = []

    _append_seed(pool, np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64), N)

    if parent_ref is not None:
        _append_seed(pool, _resample_length(parent_ref, n), N)

    alphas = (0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.7, 1.0, 1.5, 2.2, 3.0)
    for a in alphas:
        _append_seed(pool, _power_seed(n, a, False), N)
    for a in alphas:
        _append_seed(pool, _power_seed(n, a, True), N)

    steps = (
        (0.50, 0.333333),
        (0.50, 0.666667),
        (0.35, 0.25),
        (0.35, 0.55),
        (0.65, 0.75),
        (0.65, 0.45),
        (0.25, 0.15),
        (0.75, 0.85),
    )
    for a, lm in steps:
        _append_seed(pool, _step_seed(n, a, lm), N)

    random_counts = max(0, int(num_random))
    if random_counts > 0:
        K_choices = (8, 12, 16, 24)
        per = max(1, random_counts // len(K_choices))
        for K in K_choices:
            for _ in range(per):
                _append_seed(pool, _random_block_seed(n, K, rng), N)

    return pool, N


def _softmax_heights(z, K):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size == 0:
        raise ValueError("empty z")
    mm = float(np.max(z))
    z = z - mm
    H = np.exp(z)
    s = float(H.sum())
    if s <= 1e-18:
        H = np.full(K, 1.0 / K)
    else:
        H = H / s * float(K)
    return H


def _block_objective(z, n, N, q):
    """
    Smooth Lq surrogate for the block-restricted problem.
    Return approximately max(2*n*conv_coef) for the length-n vector produced
    by block heights softmax(z).
    """
    K = int(z.size)
    try:
        H = _softmax_heights(z, K)
    except Exception:
        return 1e6
    p = _heights_to_p(H, n)
    if p is None:
        return 1e6

    P = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(P * P, N)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    y = 2.0 * float(n) * c
    L = y.size

    logy = np.log(np.maximum(y, 1e-300))
    v = float(q) * logy
    m = float(np.max(v))
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return 1e6
    s = float(np.sum(np.exp(v - m)))
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return 1e6

    log_mean_q = m + math.log(s) - math.log(float(L))
    if not math.isfinite(log_mean_q):
        return 1e6
    return math.exp(log_mean_q / float(q))


def _spectral_project(ct, n, N):
    """
    Given target nonnegative convolution coefficients ct (sum 1), take a
    complex square root of its DFT, then project several nonnegative variants
    onto length-n sequences. Return the best exact-score projection.
    """
    ct = np.asarray(ct, dtype=np.float64)
    if ct.size != 2 * n - 1:
        return None, np.inf
    ct = np.maximum(ct, 0.0)
    s = float(ct.sum())
    if s <= 1e-14:
        return None, np.inf
    ct = ct / s

    C = np.fft.fft(ct, N)
    P = np.sqrt(C + 0j)
    pc = np.fft.ifft(P, N)[:n]

    best = None
    best_sc = np.inf

    variants = (
        np.real(pc),
        np.abs(np.real(pc)),
        np.abs(pc),
    )
    for arr in variants:
        cand = _normalize(arr)
        if cand is None:
            continue
        sc = _fast_score(cand, N)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best = cand
    return best, best_sc


def _spectral_candidate(p, n, N, target_y, cap_y, power_alpha=None, mode="cap"):
    """
    Build a target convolution from current p and return a spectral projection.
    mode='cap' clips coefficients to cap_y/(2n); mode='power' flattens by c**alpha.
    """
    Pft = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(Pft * Pft, N)[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

    if mode == "power" and power_alpha is not None:
        small = 1e-300
        logc = np.log(np.maximum(c, small))
        ct = np.exp(float(power_alpha) * logc)
    else:
        lower = 2.0 * float(n) / float(2 * n - 1)
        cap_y = max(float(cap_y), float(lower) + 1e-12)
        cap_coeff = cap_y / (2.0 * float(n))
        ct = np.minimum(c, cap_coeff)
        s = float(ct.sum())
        if s < 1e-12:
            ct = c
        else:
            ct = ct / s

    return _spectral_project(ct, n, N)


def _random_perturb(p, rng, sigma=0.15):
    z = rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), size=p.size)
    cand = p * np.exp(z)
    cand = np.maximum(cand, 1e-16)
    return _normalize(cand)


def _spectral_refine(p, n, N, deadline, target_y, rng):
    """
    Iterate spectral projections, gradually lowering the cap and occasionally
    using power-flattening targets. Keep the best sequence.
    """
    current = _normalize(p)
    if current is None:
        return p, np.inf
    current_y = float(_fast_score(current, N))
    best = current.copy()
    best_y = current_y

    lower = 2.0 * float(n) / float(2 * n - 1)
    target_y = max(float(target_y), lower + 1e-6)
    no_best = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        base = current
        base_y = current_y

        frac = 0.97 if it < 30 else (0.99 if base_y - target_y > 0.05 else 0.997)
        cap_y = max(target_y, base_y * frac)

        if it % 4 == 0:
            alpha = 0.8 - 0.1 * min(float(it) / 100.0, 2.0)
            cand, cyd = _spectral_candidate(
                base, n, N, target_y, cap_y, power_alpha=alpha, mode="power"
            )
        else:
            cand, cyd = _spectral_candidate(base, n, N, target_y, cap_y, mode="cap")

        if cand is not None and math.isfinite(cyd):
            if it % 3 == 0:
                blend = _normalize(0.5 * base + 0.5 * cand)
                if blend is not None:
                    bs = float(_fast_score(blend, N))
                    if bs < cyd:
                        cand, cyd = blend, bs

            if cyd < current_y or (cyd < current_y + 0.05 and it % 3 == 0):
                current = cand
                current_y = cyd

            if cyd < best_y:
                best = cand
                best_y = cyd
                no_best = 0
            else:
                no_best += 1

            if no_best > 40:
                pert = _random_perturb(best, rng, 0.15)
                if pert is not None:
                    current = pert
                    current_y = float(_fast_score(current, N))
                no_best = 0
        else:
            no_best += 1

        if no_best > 300:
            break

    return best, best_y


def _gradient_refine(p, n, N, deadline, target_y, rng):
    """
    Smoothed-max gradient ascent/descent in log variables.
    For p_i = exp(z_i)/sum, the direction for reducing a smooth Lq objective is
       z_i -= eta * H_i,
    with H_i = B_i - C, B_i = sum_k w_k p_{k-i}, C = sum_k w_k c_k.
    Multiplicative p update implements this with a trimmed step.
    """
    current = _normalize(p)
    if current is None:
        return p, np.inf
    current_y = float(_fast_score(current, N))
    best = current.copy()
    best_y = current_y

    q_list = (8, 16, 32, 64)
    no_best = 0
    it_global = 0

    for q in q_list:
        if q <= 8:
            alphas = (0.05, 0.15, 0.4, 1.0, 2.0)
        else:
            alphas = (0.02, 0.06, 0.15, 0.4, 1.0)

        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
            it_global += 1

            Pft = np.fft.rfft(current, N)
            c = np.fft.irfft(Pft * Pft, N)[:2 * n - 1]
            c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
            y = 2.0 * float(n) * c
            max_y = float(np.max(y))
            if not math.isfinite(max_y) or max_y <= 0.0:
                break

            small = 1e-300
            logw = float(q - 1) * np.log(np.maximum(y, small))
            mlog = float(np.max(logw))
            if not math.isfinite(mlog):
                break
            w = np.exp(logw - mlog)
            sw = float(w.sum())
            if sw <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(sw):
                w = np.full(c.shape, 1.0 / c.shape[0])
            else:
                w = w / sw

            Wft = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
            B = np.fft.irfft(Wft * np.conj(Pft), N)[:n]
            Cterm = float(np.dot(w, c))
            H = B - Cterm
            maxH = float(np.max(np.abs(H)))
            if not math.isfinite(maxH) or maxH < 1e-14:
                break

            best_cand = current
            best_cand_y = current_y

            for a in alphas:
                if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                    break
                eta = float(a) / maxH
                delta = -eta * H
                delta = np.clip(delta, -50.0, 50.0)
                cand = current * np.exp(delta)
                cand = np.maximum(cand, 1e-16)
                s = float(cand.sum())
                if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
                    continue
                cand = cand / s
                sc = float(_fast_score(cand, N))
                if sc < best_cand_y - 1e-12:
                    best_cand = cand
                    best_cand_y = sc

            accepted = False
            if best_cand_y < current_y - 1e-12:
                current = best_cand
                current_y = best_cand_y
                accepted = True
            elif best_cand_y < current_y + 0.04 and it_global % 5 == 0:
                current = best_cand
                current_y = best_cand_y
                accepted = True

            if current_y < best_y - 1e-12:
                best = current.copy()
                best_y = current_y
                no_best = 0
            else:
                no_best += 1

            if not accepted and no_best > 30:
                pert = _random_perturb(current, rng, 0.20)
                if pert is not None:
                    current = pert
                    current_y = float(_fast_score(current, N))
                no_best = 0

            if no_best > 350:
                break

        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break

    return best, best_y


def _polish_budget(p, n, N, rng, budget, target_y):
    """Use a budgeted mix of spectral and gradient polishing."""
    q = _normalize(p)
    if q is None:
        return p, np.inf
    y0 = float(_fast_score(q, N))
    if not math.isfinite(y0):
        return p, y0

    start = time.monotonic()
    end = start + max(0.0, float(budget))

    t1 = min(end, start + 0.45 * float(budget))
    p1, y1 = _spectral_refine(q, n, N, t1, target_y, rng)

    t2 = min(end, time.monotonic() + 0.40 * float(budget))
    p2, y2 = _gradient_refine(p1, n, N, t2, target_y, rng)

    if time.monotonic() < end - 1.0:
        p3, y3 = _spectral_refine(p2, n, N, end, target_y, rng)
        if y3 < y2:
            p2, y2 = p3, y3

    if y1 < y2:
        p2, y2 = p1, y1
    if y0 < y2:
        p2, y2 = q, y0

    return p2, float(y2)


def _choose_target(score, n):
    lower = 2.0 * float(n) / float(2 * n - 1) + 0.02
    if not math.isfinite(score):
        return max(1.45, lower)
    if score >= 1.9:
        return max(1.62, lower)
    if score >= 1.7:
        return max(1.52, lower)
    if score >= 1.55:
        return max(1.42, lower)
    if score >= 1.45:
        return max(1.35, lower)
    return max(1.25, score * 0.96, lower)


def _update_best(best, best_score, p, score):
    if p is None or not math.isfinite(score):
        return best, best_score
    if score < best_score - 1e-12:
        return p.copy(), float(score)
    return best, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for the best nonnegative coefficient sequence within the time budget.
    Returns the best normalized sequence found.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, budget - 20.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best = None
    best_score = np.inf

    # Use the supplied construction only as a seed/source.
    try:
        parent_arr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        parent_arr = None

    if parent_arr is not None:
        parent = _normalize(parent_arr)
        if parent is not None:
            ps = _safe_score(parent)
            if math.isfinite(ps):
                best = parent
                best_score = float(ps)

    if best is None:
        fallback = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        best = fallback
        best_score = _fast_score(fallback, _fft_len(1024))

    # Too little time: return a valid immediately.
    if budget < 35:
        seq = [float(x) for x in best]
        try:
            val = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
        except Exception:
            val = np.inf
        if not math.isfinite(val):
            seq = [1.0 / 1024.0] * 1024
        return seq

    # Choose primary computation length based on available time.
    if budget < 600:
        n_primary = 1024
        n_large = 2048
        num_random = 10
        do_block_opt = False
    elif budget < 1500:
        n_primary = 2048
        n_large = 4096
        num_random = 20
        do_block_opt = True
    else:
        n_primary = 4096
        n_large = 8192
        num_random = 28
        do_block_opt = True

    # Primary pool and polishing.
    pool_primary, N_primary = _build_pool(n_primary, rng, best, num_random)

    if do_block_opt and _scipy_minimize is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 500.0:
        for K in (10, 16):
            for _ in range(2):
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 320.0:
                    break
                z0 = rng.normal(0.0, 0.7, size=K)
                trend = float(rng.uniform(-0.6, 0.6))
                z0 = z0 + trend * np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, K)
                try:
                    res = _scipy_minimize(
                        _block_objective,
                        z0,
                        args=(int(n_primary), N_primary, 24),
                        method="Nelder-Mead",
                        options={"maxiter": 220, "xatol": 1e-4, "fatol": 1e-7},
                    )
                except Exception:
                    res = None
                if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
                    try:
                        H = _softmax_heights(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64), K)
                        popt = _heights_to_p(H, n_primary)
                        _append_seed(pool_primary, popt, N_primary)
                    except Exception:
                        pass

    top_primary = _sort_pool(pool_primary, 5)
    if not top_primary:
        top_primary = [(best_score, best.copy())]

    reserve_large = 450.0 if n_large > n_primary else 80.0
    main_deadline = max(time.monotonic(), deadline - reserve_large)

    idx = 0
    for seed_score, p_seed in top_primary:
        if time.monotonic() >= main_deadline:
            break
        idx += 1
        left = max(0.0, main_deadline - time.monotonic())
        budget_seed = min(360.0, max(65.0, left / max(1, len(top_primary) - idx + 1)))
        target = _choose_target(seed_score, n_primary)
        p2, y2 = _polish_budget(p_seed, n_primary, N_primary, rng, budget_seed, target)
        best, best_score = _update_best(best, best_score, p2, y2)

    # Larger length phase, if useful and time permits.
    if n_large > n_primary and time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
        N_large = _fft_len(n_large)
        pool_large = []
        _append_seed(pool_large, _resample_length(best, n_large), N_large)
        _append_seed(pool_large, _step_seed(n_large, 0.5, 1.0 / 3.0), N_large)
        _append_seed(pool_large, _step_seed(n_large, 0.5, 2.0 / 3.0), N_large)
        _append_seed(pool_large, _random_block_seed(n_large, 12, rng), N_large)
        _append_seed(pool_large, _random_block_seed(n_large, 20, rng), N_large)
        _append_seed(pool_large, _power_seed(n_large, 0.5, False), N_large)
        _append_seed(pool_large, _power_seed(n_large, 0.7, True), N_large)

        if _scipy_minimize is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 300.0:
            for K in (12, 18):
                z0 = rng.normal(0.0, 0.7, size=K)
                trend = float(rng.uniform(-0.6, 0.6))
                z0 = z0 + trend * np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, K)
                try:
                    res = _scipy_minimize(
                        _block_objective,
                        z0,
                        args=(int(n_large), N_large, 24),
                        method="Nelder-Mead",
                        options={"maxiter": 180, "xatol": 1e-4, "fatol": 1e-7},
                    )
                except Exception:
                    res = None
                if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
                    try:
                        H = _softmax_heights(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64), K)
                        popt = _heights_to_p(H, n_large)
                        _append_seed(pool_large, popt, N_large)
                    except Exception:
                        pass

        top_large = _sort_pool(pool_large, 3)
        if top_large:
            large_idx = 0
            for seed_score, p_seed in top_large:
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 30.0:
                    break
                large_idx += 1
                left = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
                budget_seed = min(620.0, max(100.0, left / max(1, len(top_large) - large_idx + 1)))
                target = _choose_target(seed_score, n_large)
                p2, y2 = _polish_budget(p_seed, n_large, N_large, rng, budget_seed, target)
                best, best_score = _update_best(best, best_score, p2, y2)

    # Final polishing of the currently best sequence at its own length.
    if best is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
        n_best = best.size
        N_best = _fft_len(n_best)
        target = _choose_target(best_score, n_best)
        budget_final = max(0.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.75)
        if budget_final >= 60.0:
            p2, y2 = _polish_budget(best, n_best, N_best, rng, budget_final, target)
            best, best_score = _update_best(best, best_score, p2, y2)

    # Final validation/fallback via the exact evaluator if available.
    seq = [float(x) for x in best]
    try:
        val = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
    except Exception:
        val = best_score

    if not math.isfinite(val):
        seq = [1.0 / 1024.0] * 1024
    return seq
```
